Second, a hierarchical metric learning (HML) strategy is embedded into HML-FSAR. Composed of center metric, alignment metric, contrastive metric, dictionary metric and prototype metric, HML imposes progressive multi-stage complementary constraints from frame-level representations to final class prototypes, so as to jointly optimize feature compactness, heterogeneous spatial-temporal alignment, inter-class discriminability and anti-noise robustness. The proposed HML-FSAR method is validated on five widely-used FSAR datasets, and experimental results fully demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [69] [Compact Neural Appearance Models for Efficient Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05255)
*Florian Hahlbohm,Jorge Condor,Linus Franke,Martin Eisemann,Marcus Magnor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对3D高斯泼溅（3D Gaussian Splatting）中基于球谐函数（SH）的外观表示与多种最新的球面外观模型进行了端到端系统比较，并提出了一种用小型共享MLP解码紧凑逐基元潜在代码的隐式神经外观表示方案，将所有模型集成到统一的优化光栅化管线中以公平评估重建质量、内存占用及性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于显式基元的辐射场（如3D高斯泼溅）通常使用低阶球谐函数（SH）建模视线相关外观。SH虽评估高效，但其系数占据逐基元存储和内存流量的主导地位，且带限基函数限制了角度细节表达能力。作者希望通过系统性比较和提出更紧凑的替代方案，为超越SH的外观建模提供实践指导。

Method: （1）在统一优化的管线上对SH与多种近期球面外观模型进行全面端到端对比；（2）提出隐式神经外观模型：使用小型的共享MLP解码紧凑的逐基元潜在编码；（3）将所有模型的前向与反向传播融合到可微CUDA光栅化器中；（4）提供可移植的WebGL查看器以支持笔记本和移动GPU环境。

Result: 近期的球面外观模型在整体质量-效率权衡上表现最佳；提出的神经表示是最紧凑的模型，相比三阶SH将逐基元外观占用从192字节降至28字节，优化速度提升1.3倍，同时重建质量得到改善。此外，分析揭示了外观参数化对优化过程的塑造作用，包括恢复几何的差异以及表达力强的模型易吸收非静态场景内容。

Conclusion: 通过统一的框架和深入分析，本文为在图像指标之外替换传统球谐函数提供了实用指导，表明更先进的外观参数化不仅能减少存储和加速优化，还能提升重建质量，且隐式神经方案在存储效率上具有显著优势。

Abstract: Explicit primitive-based radiance fields such as 3D Gaussian Splatting typically model view-dependent appearance using low-order spherical harmonics (SH). Although efficient to evaluate, SH coefficients dominate per-primitive storage and memory traffic, while their band-limited basis restricts angular detail. We present a thorough, end-to-end comparison of SH and recent spherical appearance models and introduce an implicit alternative that decodes compact per-primitive latent codes using a tiny shared MLP. We integrate all models into the same optimized pipeline, fusing their forward and backward passes into a differentiable CUDA rasterizer and provide a portable WebGL viewer for laptop and mobile GPUs. Our evaluation across reconstruction quality, memory use, and optimization and rendering performance shows that recent spherical models offer the strongest overall quality-efficiency trade-off. Our neural representation is the most compact model evaluated and, compared to third-degree SH, reduces the per-primitive appearance footprint from 192 to 28 bytes, accelerates optimization by 1.3$\times$, while improving reconstruction quality. We further analyze how appearance parametrization shapes optimization, identifying differences in recovered geometry and the tendency of expressive models to absorb non-static scene content. Together, our framework and analysis provide practical guidance for replacing SH beyond what image metrics alone can capture.

</details>


### [70] [Learning Spatial-Spectral Refinement and Calibrating Complementary Observations for Hyperspectral Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05303)
*Liqian Yang,Xingchi Chen,Xinfeng Gui,Xiangyong Cao,Qianxin Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TSR-ITNR框架，一种基于隐式张量神经表示的两阶段自监督高光谱与多光谱图像融合方法，通过表示精炼和观测引导校准来重建高分辨率高光谱图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于隐式神经表示（INRs）的HMIF方法未能充分捕获细粒度空间结构和丰富的光谱依赖性，且LR-HSI与HR-MSI主要通过退化一致性约束进行融合，互补信息未得到充分利用。

Method: 提出TSR-ITNR统一自监督框架，包含两个阶段：Stage 1学习隐式Tucker表示并精炼低秩空间系数张量和光谱基，利用固定预训练去噪器提供深度先验；Stage 2通过无参数校准从两种观测中推导互补且无干扰的修正信息，恢复Stage 1中未充分捕获的信息。理论分析建立了光谱精炼的几何保持性质和校准的正交互补性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在无需真实高分辨率HSI监督的情况下，取得了优异的定量、视觉和光谱重建性能，并在下游语义分割任务中进一步验证了有效性。

Conclusion: TSR-ITNR通过两阶段设计有效解决了现有INR方法在空间细节捕获和光谱依赖性建模方面的不足，同时充分利用了两种观测的互补信息，为无监督HMIF提供了一种新的理论支撑和实用方案。

Abstract: Hyperspectral and multispectral image fusion (HMIF) aims to reconstruct a high-resolution hyperspectral image (HR-HSI) by combining the fine spatial details of a high-resolution multispectral image (HR-MSI) with the rich spectral information of a low-resolution hyperspectral image (LR-HSI). Recent advances in implicit neural representations (INRs) have enabled flexible coordinate-based modeling for HMIF; however, existing INR-based approaches may not fully capture fine-grained spatial structures and rich spectral dependencies. Moreover, the LR-HSI and HR-MSI are primarily incorporated through degradation-consistency constraints, leaving their complementary information underexploited. To address these limitations, we propose Two-Stage Reconstruction with Implicit Tensor Neural Representation (TSR-ITNR), a unified self-supervised framework integrating representation refinement and observation-guided calibration. In Stage 1, TSR-ITNR learns an implicit Tucker representation and refines its low-rank spatial coefficient tensor and spectral basis to better capture fine spatial structures and interband correlations. A fixed pretrained denoiser further provides a deep prior for the preliminary reconstruction. In Stage 2, parameter-free calibration derives complementary and noninterfering corrections from both observations to recover information insufficiently captured in Stage 1. Theoretical analysis establishes the geometry-preserving property of spectral refinement and the orthogonal complementarity of calibration. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate strong quantitative, visual, and spectral reconstruction performance without ground-truth HR-HSI supervision. Beyond conventional reconstruction metrics, we further assess the effectiveness of TSR-ITNR using downstream semantic segmentation accuracy.

</details>


### [71] [Adaptive Gated Deepfake Detection for Low-Resolution and Resource-Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05320)
*Vaishnavi Sen,Cody Laurie,Rashida Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaGate-DF，一个自适应门控的深度伪造检测框架，通过图像质量线索将样本路由到双多出口系统，使得高质量图像可以提前退出从而节省计算开销。在Celeb-DF和FaceForensics++两个基准数据集上，AdaGate-DF展现出检测性能、不确定性感知预测与计算效率之间的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测模型通常依赖高质量输入、固定的推理路径和高计算开销的架构，这限制了它们在低分辨率和资源受限场景下的应用，需要一种能够自适应处理变质量输入的高效检测方案。

Method: AdaGate-DF采用自适应门控机制，基于图像质量线索将样本路由到双多出口（dual multi-exit）系统，高质量图像能够在较早的出口退出，从而减少不必要的计算负载。

Result: 在Celeb-DF数据集上，AdaGate-DF达到AUC 0.9370，优于MaD-CoRN和DefakeHop++，且保持低推理延迟；在384×384分辨率下AUC提升至0.9708。在FaceForensics++上，该方法在类别不平衡条件下依然保持有效性。

Conclusion: AdaGate-DF在检测性能、不确定性感知预测和计算效率之间取得了实际可用的平衡，适用于可变质量的深度伪造检测场景，尤其对低分辨率和资源受限环境具有实用价值。

Abstract: Deepfake detection models often rely on high-quality inputs, fixed inference paths, and computationally expensive architectures, limiting their use in low-resolution and resource-constrained settings. This paper proposes AdaGate-DF, an adaptive gated deepfake detection framework that uses image-quality cues to route samples through a dual multi-exit system so high-quality images can exit earlier and save compute. We evaluated AdaGate-DF against MaD-CoRN, DefakeHop++, and ShuffleNetV2 on two benchmark datasets (Celeb-DF and FaceForensics++) under multiple configurations to test image resolution dependence and training and inference efficiency. On Celeb-DF, AdaGate-DF achieves an AUC of 0.9370, outperforming MaD-CoRN and DefakeHop++ while maintaining a low inference latency. Resolution-based testing shows consistent improvement as input resolution increases, reaching an AUC of 0.9708 at 384 by 384. The FaceForensics++ results highlight that AdaGate-DF remains effective under class imbalance, following competitive results with evaluated models. Overall, AdaGate-DF demonstrated a practical balance between detection performance, uncertainty-aware prediction, and computational efficiency for variable-quality deepfake detection.

</details>


### [72] [Scalable Detection of Fossil Palynomorphs in Multifocal Digital Microscopy Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05323)
*Abbas Shaikh,Praise Mayor,Patrick Ainlay-Vazquez,Aditya Viswanathan,Teon Golden,Eric Zhang,Ingrid C. Romero,Alexander E. White,Scott Wing,Arko Barman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个可扩展的全自动孢粉学检测端到端流水线，通过图像分解压缩、现代目标检测模型（RF-DETR，AP@50达0.879）、大规模输出合成算法和I/O优化，将单个玻片样本的孢粉检测时间从数天手动检查缩短至—小时内的自动分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孢粉（花粉、孢子、沟鞭藻等）是古气候和古生态系统研究的重要高分辨率记录，但现有检测方法依赖人工分析高分辨率多焦点数字显微镜图像，速度慢且耗时，迫使研究者在研究规模上做出妥协，严重制约了孢粉学研究的大规模开展。

Method: 提出端到端可扩展流水线：(1)将数字化的多焦点显微镜全玻片图像高效分解压缩为可处理的2D瓦片；(2)基准测试包括RF-DETR在内的现代目标检测模型，实现孢粉检测；(3)设计高效算法合成大规模高分辨率图像上的检测输出；(4)通过I/O优化加速推理时间。

Result: 在孢粉检测任务上实现AP@50达到0.879的检测精度；端到端流水线将单个玻片的孢粉检测时间从数天人工检查缩短到—小时内自动完成，大幅提升研究规模。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了全玻片图像的自动化孢粉检测，显著降低人工成本和时间开销，为孢粉学研究在更大尺度上的开展提供了可行性，是古气候和古生态系统研究的重要技术推动。

Abstract: Palynomorphs (microscopic, organic-walled fossils such as pollen, spores, and dinoflagellates) are important high-resolution records of past climates and are critical to the study of ancient ecosystems. Existing methods rely on manual analysis of high-resolution, multifocal digital microscopy images, which is slow and time-consuming and requires researchers to compromise on the scale of their investigations. To the best of our knowledge, our work proposes the first ever scalable end-to-end pipeline for automated palynomorph detection in whole slide images that addresses this bottleneck through: (1) efficient methods for decomposing and compressing digitized multifocal microscope slide images into tractable 2-dimensional tiles for analysis; (2) benchmarking modern object detection models, including RF-DETR, for the detection of palynomorphs, achieving an AP@50 of 0.879; (3) an efficient algorithm for the synthesis of detection outputs across large-scale, high-resolution images; and (4) an I/O optimization resulting in faster inference time. Our methods drastically reduce the time required for palynomorph detection in a single slide from often days of manual inspection to under one hour of automated analysis, enabling palynological research at a substantially greater scale.

</details>


### [73] [Lightweight Vision Transformer Compression for On-Device Plant Disease Detection in Resource-Constrained Agricultural Field Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05334)
*Mahadev Sunil Kumar,Bhavika Gondi,Desaisetty Venkata Satya Sai Swapnith,Gangireddy Rahul Jogi,Sudheesh Manalil,Arnab Raha,Amitava Mukherjee,Parthasarathy Seethapathy,G. Gopakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的Vision Transformer（ViT）压缩框架，结合Hessian平衡自适应块剪枝（H-BAC）、量化和基于注意力的知识蒸馏技术，应用于印度辣椒病害分类任务。在跨村跨设备的真实分布外测试集上，压缩模型实现了54.5倍的大小缩减（从327.42MB降至6.01MB），同时保持与95.13% FP32基线相当甚至更优的分类精度。然而，对比实验显示，直接训练同尺寸学生模型可达94.87%的精度，表明剪枝和蒸馏在中小型模型场景下的计算成本收益仍需审慎评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辣椒是印度重要的经济作物，其病害识别依赖专家且难以自动化。ViT虽分类精度高但计算开销大，难以部署于资源受限设备。现有压缩方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏）通常孤立使用，缺乏对其组合效应及交互作用的系统探索，亟需统一框架以应对真实农业场景中的部署约束。

Method: 提出统一ViT压缩框架：1）Hessian-Balanced Adaptive Block Pruning（H-BAC），利用二阶敏感性估计指导自适应块剪枝；2）结合量化和基于注意力的知识蒸馏；3）各技术先通过受控消融实验独立评估，再将最优组件集成到面向真实农业需求的顺序部署流水线中。

Result: 在辣椒3类村庄划分数据集上，压缩模型达到或超过95.13% FP32基线的精度，模型大小缩减74-98%。完全集成的压缩流水线实现54.5倍大小缩减（327.42MB降至6.01MB），精度为95.13±2.32%。直接训练同尺寸学生模型（不经过剪枝和蒸馏）在6.01MB INT8大小下也能达到94.87%的精度，说明在中小型模型场景下，复杂压缩手段的增量收益有限。

Conclusion: 该统一压缩框架在精度保持与模型大小缩减方面展现了工程有效性，但诚实的对比实验揭示了关键洞察：在最终模型规模较小的场景下，复杂的剪枝与蒸馏流程相比直接训练小模型，精度提升并不显著（95.13% vs 94.87%），其计算成本的额外投入需要更严格的成本-收益论证。

Abstract: Chilli (Capsicum annuum) is one of India's most economically significant crops, yet its productivity is persistently threatened by diseases that are difficult to identify without expert intervention. While Vision Transformers (ViTs) have achieved high classification accuracy, their large computational footprint makes deployment on resource constrained devices challenging. Existing compression approaches typically address pruning, quantization, and knowledge distillation in isolation, leaving the potential benefits and interactions of their combined application insufficiently explored. We propose a unified Vision Transformer compression framework that combines Hessian-Balanced Adaptive Block Pruning (H-BAC), guided by second-order sensitivity estimation, with quantization and attention-based knowledge distillation. To systematically identify the most effective configuration within each compression family, each technique is first evaluated independently through controlled ablation studies, after which the best-performing components are integrated into a sequential deployment pipeline tailored to real-world agricultural constraints. On a chilli 3-class village-split dataset with a genuine cross-village, cross-device out-of-distribution test split, the resulting compressed models match or exceed the 95.13% FP32 baseline's accuracy, alongside 74-98% model size reduction, and the fully integrated compression pipeline achieves a 54.5x size reduction (327.42 MB to 6.01 MB) at 95.13 +/- 2.32% accuracy across four tested configurations. A direct comparison further reveals that, on this dataset, a directly-trained student of the same final size, without pruning or distillation, reaches comparable accuracy of 94.87%, at the same 6.01 MB INT8 size, indicating where H-BAC and knowledge distillation are, and are not yet shown to be, worth their computational cost.

</details>


### [74] [MEOX: Compact Multimodal Mixture-of-Experts for Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05351)
*Mohanad Albughdadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEOX是一个仅含311.5万参数的多模态地球观测掩码自编码器，通过传感器特定适配器、显式有效性信号和共享稀疏专家块实现异构传感器与缺失观测的灵活表征学习，在GEO-Bench六个任务上展现出超越CSMoE的迁移性能，同时保持极小的参数预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球观测表征学习方法面对异构传感器和缺失观测时，往往依赖更大的模型架构来提升能力，导致参数规模不断膨胀。作者希望探索一种更紧凑的方案，在不牺牲多模态建模能力的前提下，实现传感器灵活的表征学习与高效的任务迁移。

Method: MEOX采用多模态掩码自编码器架构，编码器仅293.9万参数。具体包括：传感器特定适配器和显式有效性信号保留模态专属处理；共享稀疏专家块实现跨模态共享，同时通过共享专家投影加私有低秩残差控制参数量；四个元数据token伴随单一空间序列通过十四个编码器块；采用旋转注意力以支持与预训练不同的下游空间网格。预训练使用122.8万MMEarth64样本，采用模态平衡掩码重建和结构化传感器随机丢弃策略。

Result: 模型在六项GEO-Bench任务上以64和224像素两种分辨率进行冻结迁移评估。腰果分割在64像素下达到64.42%平均IoU，EuroSAT在224像素下达到90.56%平均准确率，均超过报告中CSMoE的对应结果。BigEarthNet微调达到72.95%微平均精度。路由诊断区分了专家参与度、空间依赖、模态关联和功能贡献。WorldCover探测显示元数据带来0.64个百分点的增益，检索实验分离了同传感器语义与跨传感器对齐。

Conclusion: MEOX证明通过精心设计的架构——包括传感器特定适配、稀疏专家共享和元数据注入——可以用极小的参数预算实现传感器灵活的表征学习和强大的下游任务迁移，为紧凑型多模态地球观测模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Earth Observation representation learning accommodate heterogeneous sensors and missing observations, often through larger architectures. We present MEOX (Multimodal Earth Observation with eXperts), a multimodal masked autoencoder with a 2.939 million-parameter encoder and 3.115 million parameters in total. Sensor-specific adapters, explicit validity signals, and a shared sparse-expert block preserve modality-dependent processing before a learned patch-wise fusion. Four metadata tokens then accompany a single spatial sequence through fourteen further encoder blocks. Shared expert projections with private low-rank residuals constrain parameter growth, while rotary attention supports downstream spatial grids different from pretraining. The model is pretrained on 1.228 million MMEarth64 samples using modality-balanced masked reconstruction and structured sensor dropout. Frozen transfer is evaluated on six GEO-Bench tasks at both 64 and 224 pixels. The model reaches 64.42% mean intersection-over-union on cashew segmentation at 64 pixels and 90.56% average accuracy on EuroSAT at 224 pixels, exceeding the corresponding reported CSMoE results. BigEarthNet finetuning reaches 72.95% micro-average precision. Routing diagnostics distinguish expert participation, spatial dependence, modality association, and functional contribution. A held-out WorldCover probe measures a 0.64-percentage-point benefit from metadata, while retrieval separates same-sensor semantics from cross-sensor alignment. These results demonstrate sensor-flexible representation learning and strong task transfer using a compact parameter budget.

</details>


### [75] [Reflection-aware Generative Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05382)
*GeonU Kim,Shin Dong-Yeon,Tae-Hyun Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ref-GeNVS是一种无需训练的、感知反射的生成式新视角合成方法，专门针对镜子场景。其核心思想是将镜像视为两个互补视图，通过估计镜面平面并反射相机位姿形成虚拟视图，结合Mirror-gated attention和Reflection injection两阶段生成方法，在多视角扩散模型中显式利用反射关系，实现反射一致的新视角生成，无需微调即在合成和真实镜子场景中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多视角扩散模型的新视角合成方法往往无法识别场景中的镜子，也不能利用镜面反射内容进行场景生成，导致镜子场景下的生成结果缺乏反射一致性。现有方法需要额外训练来解决该问题，代价高昂。

Method: 将镜像视为两个互补视图：先估计镜面平面并反射相机位姿形成虚拟视图；在此基础上提出两阶段生成方法，包括Mirror-gated attention（镜像门控注意力）和Reflection injection（反射注入），在多视角扩散模型中显式利用反射关系实现反射一致性的新视角合成。整体方法无需训练，继承了多视角扩散模型后端的强泛化能力。

Result: 在包含镜子的合成场景和真实场景上，Ref-GeNVS优于最近的生成式NVS方法，能够生成反射一致且上下文连贯的新视图，并揭示仅通过镜子可见的场景结构。

Conclusion: 通过将镜像视为互补视图并在多视角扩散模型中显式建模反射关系，无需额外训练即可实现高质量、反射一致的镜子场景新视角合成，显示出良好的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: We propose Ref-GeNVS, a training-free, reflection-aware method for generative novel view synthesis (NVS) in mirror scenes. Existing multi-view diffusion models often fail to recognize the mirror in the scene and cannot exploit reflected content for scene generation. To fix this issue without additional training, our key idea is to treat a mirror image as two complementary views. From input images, we estimate the mirror plane and reflect camera poses to form virtual views. Based on this virtual view setup, we propose a two-stage generation method consisting of Mirror-gated attention and Reflection injection, which enables reflection-consistent NVS by explicitly leveraging reflection relationships in a multi-view diffusion model. Ref-GeNVS inherits the strong generalizability of the multi-view diffusion backbone, while it does not require finetuning. On synthetic and real scenes including mirrors, Ref-GeNVS outperforms recent generative NVS methods by generating reflection-consistent and contextually coherent novel views, revealing scene structure visible only through mirrors. Project page: https://kim-geonu.github.io/Ref-GeNVS/

</details>


### [76] [Think-Verify-Revise: Neuro-Symbolic Visual Reasoning with Vision-Language Models and Dynamic Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05388)
*Homayoun Afshari,Pietro Basci,Alessandro Russo,Lia Morra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号（NeSy）框架，通过视觉语言模型（VLM）自动归纳一阶逻辑规则，并利用动态逻辑张量网络（D-LTN）进行可微规则验证，两者形成闭环迭代反馈（Think-Verify-Revise）。在ViSudo-PC基准的四个视觉域上，仅用三个训练样本即成功归纳出有效的数独约束规则，AUC指标达到或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉推理任务需要同时感知视觉内容并应用形式化关系约束，但纯神经网络和纯符号方法在孤立使用时都无法很好地解决这一问题。现有方法缺乏将两者紧密耦合的自动化规则发现机制。

Method: 提出一个闭环迭代的神经符号框架：1）VLM基于少量标注视觉示例提出符合严格语法的一阶逻辑候选规则（Think）；2）运行时根据规则自动组装D-LTN并对CNN生成的视觉嵌入进行规则验证（Verify）；3）将验证失败信息反馈给VLM以引导下一轮假设修正（Revise）。

Result: 在ViSudo-PC基准的四个视觉域（MNIST、EMNIST、KMNIST、FMNIST）上，该系统仅使用三个训练示例作为视觉上下文即可归纳出有效的数独约束规则，AUC分数匹配或超越NeuPSL和LTN等现有方法。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了通过VLM实现自动规则发现的潜力，NeSy框架有效弥合了视觉感知与形式推理之间的鸿沟，且代码已开源供复现。

Abstract: Visual reasoning tasks require a system to jointly perceive visual content and apply formal relational constraints---a combination that neither pure neural nor purely symbolic approaches handle well in isolation. This paper proposes a Neuro-Symbolic (NeSy) framework that closes this gap by tightly coupling a Vision-Language Model (VLM) for automatic First-Order Logic (FOL) rule induction with a Dynamic Logic Tensor Network (D-LTN) for differentiable rule verification, in a closed iterative feedback loop. The VLM receives a small set of labelled visual examples and proposes candidate FOL rules conforming to a strict grammar (Think); the D-LTN is automatically assembled from these rules at runtime and evaluates them grounding on CNN-produced visual embeddings (Verify); and verification failures are fed back to guide the VLM's next hypothesis (Revise). Evaluated on the ViSudo-PC benchmark across four visual domains (MNIST, EMNIST, KMNIST, FMNIST), the system induces valid Sudoku constraint rules using only three training examples as visual context. The proposed method achieves AUC scores matching or outperforming previous methods (NeuPSL, LTN), showing the potential for automatic rule discovery through VLM. Code is available at https://github.com/homayoun-afshari/nesy.

</details>


### [77] [CrossDepth: Geometry-Constrained Attention for Generalizable Multi-View Surround Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05397)
*Samer Abualhanud,Max Mehltretter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CrossDepth，一种用于自动驾驶多视角环绕相机的自监督深度估计方法。该方法通过基于像素的相机感知光线嵌入处理相机内参差异，并通过受几何约束的跨图像注意力扩展像素上下文，解决跨图像深度不一致问题，在DDAD和nuScenes数据集上优于现有自监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角环绕相机中相邻图像重叠极小，大多数像素深度必须依赖单目外观线索推断，而不同图像间的外观线索在相机内参差异和有限感受野的影响下容易被不一致地解读，导致跨图像深度估计不一致。

Method: 1) 使用逐像素相机感知光线嵌入来条件化特征，使网络能够处理相机内参差异带来的单目线索变化；2) 通过标定装置约束的几何可行区域进行跨图像注意力，将每个像素的上下文扩展到自身图像之外；3) 基于光度一致性以完全自监督方式训练。

Result: 在DDAD和nuScenes数据集上的域内和跨域评估中，深度估计准确性和跨图像深度一致性均优于现阶段自监督方法。

Conclusion: 通过同时解决相机内参差异和有限感受野两大跨图像不一致来源，CrossDepth有效提升了自监督多视角深度估计的整体精度与跨视图一致性。

Abstract: Reliable 3D understanding of the surrounding environment is a core requirement for autonomous driving. Multi-view surround camera rigs provide broad scene coverage, but the spatially adjacent images typically overlap only minimally. Consequently, the depth of most pixels must be inferred from monocular appearance cues. These cues can appear differently across images and may therefore be interpreted differently by the depth estimation model. We target two main sources of cross-image inconsistency: differences in camera intrinsics and the limited receptive field of each image. We address the former by conditioning the features on per-pixel camera-aware ray embeddings, enabling the network to account for camera-dependent variations in monocular cues. We address the latter by extending each pixel's context beyond its own image through cross-image attention constrained to geometrically plausible regions, derived from the calibrated rig setup. The model is trained in a fully self-supervised manner based on photometric consistency. Evaluations on DDAD and nuScenes show improved overall depth accuracy and cross-image depth consistency over state-of-the-art self-supervised methods under in-domain and cross-domain evaluation. Code is available at https://abualhanud.github.io/CrossDepthPage/.

</details>


### [78] [From Interpretability Methods to Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05399)
*Julien Colin,Nuria Oliver,Thomas Serre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文呼吁可解释AI（XAI）领域从"方法为中心"转向"模型为中心"，聚焦于两个互补方向：一是利用现有工具描述和比较不同模型所表征与计算的内容；二是研究模型能否被依赖它的人类真正理解——这只能通过独立评估者的实测来衡量，而非推理推断。作者认为工具集已足够成熟，并提出了一个模型中心的XAI研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XAI十多年来投入了大量精力构建和比较方法，却忽略了这些方法最初要回答的根本问题：模型本身有多可解释？随着模型演化我们是否在进步？现有工作几乎不关注对不同模型的可理解性进行比较，且对"人类能否真正理解模型"这一核心问题严重忽视。

Method: 本文为综述与立场论文，通过审视现有XAI工具箱（归因、特征可视化、概念基与电路基方法）的成熟度，梳理模型间比较研究的薄弱现状，并借鉴系统神经科学的研究范式，论证模型中心XAI的可行性，最后提出研究议程。

Result: 指出XAI工具箱已足够成熟以支持模型间的表征与计算比较；但模型可理解性的实证测量研究极为稀缺，现有工作多由"专家确认已有预期"主导，缺少独立评估者的视角。作者以系统神经科学为类比，说明跨模型比较与人类可理解性测量在方法论上具有可行性。

Conclusion: XAI领域重心应从方法论构建转向对模型本身的评估，包括可测量、可比较的表征差异，以及面向独立人类评估者的可理解性实证测量——后者只能通过实验测得，不能凭推理推断。作者呼吁建立模型中心XAI的研究议程。

Abstract: More than a decade in, explainable AI (XAI) for computer vision has assembled a mature toolbox: attribution, feature visualization, concept-based, and circuit-based methods. Yet almost all of the field's effort has gone into building and comparing these methods, and little into the question they were meant to answer---how interpretable are our models, and are we making progress as they evolve? We argue for shifting the field's focus from methods to models, along two complementary lines. One is already within reach: existing tools let us characterize and compare what different models represent and compute. The other is harder, and largely neglected: whether a model can actually be understood by the humans who rely on it---the independent evaluators on whom trust and certification depend, not the experts confirming what they already expect. It can only be measured, not inferred. We review why the toolbox is mature enough to support both, survey the thin body of work comparing models, draw a parallel to systems neuroscience, and close with a model-centric XAI agenda.

</details>


### [79] [A Generalizable Feature Extractor for Alzheimer's-Related Brain MRI Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05400)
*Reza Rajabli,D. Louis Collins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了一个用于脑龄预测的紧凑型3D CNN（冻结718万参数）作为神经影像基础模型的可行性，通过LoRA仅需约1%的可训练参数即可适配多种阿尔茨海默病相关下游任务。在六项实验中，模型在ADNI、OASIS-3等数据集上表现出色，包括痴呆分类（AUC 0.964）、跨数据集迁移（AUC 0.871）、MCI进展预测（AUC 0.828）、淀粉样蛋白阳性预测（AUC 0.804）以及海马体和白质高信号体积估计（R² 0.80和0.91），表明脑龄模型可成为有效的可复用基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法在神经影像领域的应用常受限于标注数据不足的问题，迁移学习虽被认为有帮助，但其有效性尤其在阿尔茨海默病研究中的表现尚不明确。作者希望探究一个紧凑的监督预训练模型能否作为可复用的基础模型，并验证其是否能直接在未见过的数据集上工作而无需针对每个任务重新训练。

Method: 冻结一个先前为脑龄预测训练的3D CNN的718万权重作为骨干网络，利用低秩适配（LoRA）技术为每个下游任务仅引入约1%的可训练参数进行适配。设计六项实验系统评估通用性：ADNI痴呆分类（实验1）、在OASIS-3上的零样本跨数据集迁移（实验2）、结合输出logit与年龄和认知得分区分稳定型与进展型MCI（实验3）、基于结构MRI预测淀粉样蛋白阳性（实验4）、以及分别估计ICV归一化的海马体和白质高信号体积（实验5和6）。

Result: 实验结果显示：ADNI上区分认知正常与痴呆达到AUC 0.964；模型无需重训练直接应用于OASIS-3达到AUC 0.871；区分稳定型与进展型MCI的AUC为0.828；从结构MRI预测淀粉样蛋白阳性AUC为0.804；海马体和白质高信号体积估计的R²分别达到0.80和0.91，后者通常需要更大的U-Net网络才能完成。

Conclusion: 一个以脑龄为监督信号训练的紧凑模型可以作为阿尔茨海默病相关任务的有效基础模型，仅需约1%的额外参数即可适配各任务，且能在无任何训练的情况下迁移到未见过的队列。这表明即使在严格的数据约束下，精心训练的脑龄模型也具有良好的可复用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: When there is not enough labeled data to properly train deep learning models, transfer learning can help. We still do not fully understand how effective it is in neuroimaging, especially for Alzheimer's disease research. It is also not clear if these transferred models can work on new datasets without being retrained for each specific task. We evaluate whether a compact, supervised pretrained model can serve as a reusable foundation model for downstream neuroimaging tasks. We freeze the 7.18 million weights of a 3D CNN previously trained for brain-age prediction, and adapt it to each task using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), requiring only ~1% additional trainable parameters. We evaluate generalizability in six experiments. Adapting the model to classify cognitively normal versus Dementia on ADNI gave an AUC of 0.964 on held-out folds (Experiment #1). Applying that adapted model unchanged to OASIS-3, with no retraining, gave an AUC of 0.871 (Experiment #2). Reusing its output logit together with age and a cognitive score distinguished stable from progressing MCI with an AUC of 0.828 (Experiment #3). Adapting the same backbone to predict amyloid positivity from structural MRI gave an AUC of 0.804 (Experiment #4). Finally, the same approach estimated ICV-normalized hippocampal and white matter hypointensity volumes directly from the T1w image, with R^2 of 0.80 and 0.91 respectively, tasks normally addressed with much larger U-Net networks (Experiments #5 and #6). A compact model supervised on brain age can therefore serve as a reusable backbone, adapting to each task with ~1% additional parameters and transferring to an unseen cohort without any training. Our findings suggest that a carefully trained brain age model can serve as an effective foundation model for Alzheimer's related tasks, even under strict data constraints.

</details>


### [80] [UniMate: One Unified Model to Animate Diverse Skeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05415)
*Linzhan Mou,Jiahui Lei,Zhiyang Dou,Chenyue Cai,Chaoyue Song,Adam Finkelstein,Szymon Rusinkiewicz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniMate是一个统一的骨架动画基础模型，能够仅根据3D资产和文本提示为任意骨架生成关节运动，无需测试时优化或逐骨架重训练，通过拓扑感知扩散Transformer架构与大规模多物种数据集实现零样本跨拓扑迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化蒙皮技术已能大规模生成动画就绪的3D资产，但驱动这些资产的运动生成仍是瓶颈。现有学习方法受限于拓扑结构，依赖类别特定模板或需要推理时的骨架微调和参考运动，缺乏通用的跨骨架动画生成能力。

Method: UniMate提出拓扑感知扩散Transformer，通过三种机制将骨架拓扑融入注意力：(1) 基于成对关节关系和测地距离的图感知注意力偏置；(2) 通过图拉普拉斯将RoPE推广到任意运动树的谱旋转位置编码；(3) 从静态骨架注意力池化的全局拓扑条件器。同时构建了包含13,006条运动序列的UniML3D数据集，涵盖两足、四足、鸟类、海洋、昆虫、蛇形和关节刚体等多类骨架，并进行统一归一化和文本配对。

Result: 在质量、泛化性和效率方面均优于最先进的基线方法，并支持零样本跨拓扑迁移、中间帧插值、运动扩展和文本引导编辑等多任务能力。

Conclusion: UniMate证明了通过拓扑感知设计与大规模多物种数据训练，可以实现真正通用的任意骨架文本驱动动画生成，为动画制作和3D内容创作提供了高效的基础解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in automatic rigging now deliver animation-ready 3D assets at scale, yet generating the motion to drive them remains a bottleneck. Existing learned animators are topology-constrained: they rely on category-specific templates or require per-skeleton fine-tuning and reference motions at inference. We present UniMate, a unified foundation model that synthesizes articulated motion for arbitrary skeletons from a rigged 3D asset and a text prompt, with no test-time optimization or per-skeleton retraining. UniMate introduces a topology-aware diffusion transformer, which integrates skeletal topology into attention via three mechanisms: (1) a graph-aware attention bias from pairwise joint relations and geodesic distances; (2) a spectral rotary position embedding generalizing RoPE to arbitrary kinematic trees via the graph Laplacian; and (3) a global topological conditioner attention-pooled from the rest-pose skeleton. We also curate UniML3D, 13,006 motion sequences spanning bipedal, quadrupedal, avian, marine, insectoid, serpentine, and articulated rigid objects with unified canonicalization and text pairing. Trained on this dataset, UniMate outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in quality, generalization, and efficiency, and supports zero-shot cross-topology transfer, in-betweening, expansion, and text-guided editing. Our project page is available at https://linzhanmou.com/unimate/.

</details>


### [81] [WorldSculpt: Generating Compositional Worlds from Grounded Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05416)
*Muyao Niu,Jixuan He,Ruihan Yu,Lian Fu,Yonghao Yu,Zheng-Hui Huang,Yifan Zhan,Fengbo Lan,Yongtao Ge,Yinqiang Zheng,Kaipeng Zhang,Zhixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强单物体3D生成先验的组合式3D场景生成方法，通过多视角条件路径将单物体生成模型扩展至包含数百个物体的杂乱场景，无需场景级训练即可泛化，并引入了UE-MeshyScene基准数据集进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下游应用（如游戏、AR/VR、仿真和机器人）需要将杂乱的多物体场景表示为共享世界坐标系中的单个物体网格集合。然而在密集杂乱场景中，物体相互严重遮挡，每个视角只能看到物体几何的一小部分。现有几何方法将场景重建为单一表示，遮挡区域几何不完整；而带生成先验的组合方法仅限于简单场景。

Method: 以Pixal3D为基础，通过多视角条件路径将单物体3D生成模型扩展到多视角观察。模型仅在规范空间中的单物体数据上进行微调，却无需场景级训练即可泛化到具有严重遮挡的大型场景。

Result: 在单物体、受控多物体和UE-MeshyScene三个评估场景中，方法一致优于现有方法，且场景复杂度与遮挡程度增加时优势更大。同时可将生成的3DGS世界（如Marble和HY-World 2.0）转换为组合式网格场景。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了"从强单物体3D先验到复杂多物体场景组合生成"这一范式的可行性和可扩展性，为密集杂乱场景的组合式重建提供了新的解决思路，且无需昂贵的场景级训练数据。

Abstract: We study the problem of generating a compositional 3D representation of a cluttered scene containing hundreds of objects. The goal is to represent the scene as a collection of individual object meshes placed in a shared world frame, as required by downstream applications such as gaming, AR/VR, simulation, and robotics. This task is challenging in densely cluttered scenes, where objects heavily occlude one another and each view reveals only a fraction of their geometry. Geometry-based approaches typically reconstruct the scene as a single representation and leave incomplete geometry in occluded regions, while existing compositional methods with generative priors are largely limited to relatively simple scenes. We show that complex scenes with hundreds of objects can instead be generated compositionally by adapting a strong single-object 3D generative prior to multi-view observations. We instantiate this paradigm with Pixal3D, extending it with a multi-view conditioning pathway that grounds object generation in multiple posed observations. Although the model is finetuned entirely on single objects in canonical space, it generalizes to large scenes with severe occlusion without any scene-level training, demonstrating the feasibility and scalability of this paradigm. We further introduce UE-MeshyScene, a photorealistic benchmark of densely cluttered scenes with hundreds of objects, per-object annotations, and ground-truth meshes. Across single-object, controlled multi-object, and UE-MeshyScene evaluations, our method consistently outperforms prior approaches, with larger gains as scene complexity and occlusion increase. Finally, we demonstrate broader applicability by converting generated 3DGS worlds, such as Marble and HY-World 2.0, into compositional mesh scenes.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [82] [How Much Does Corpus Choice Change Dependency-Distance Estimates?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04223)
*Sirui Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究基于Universal Dependencies v2.18的38对同语言树库，采用一致性相关、Bland-Altman分析和12种规格的多宇宙设计，检验依赖距离估计是否可作为稳定的语言属性。结果显示跨树库一致性仅为中等水平：替换树库会逆转近40%的语言对排序，树库选择解释了约29%的组间方差。但所有树库均确认了依赖长度最小化现象。结论认为平均依赖距离更像是一个受语料条件制约的复合指标，而非稳定的语言级参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，基于单一语料库估算的依赖距离被视为语言的内在属性，但这一假设从未在独立编制的多个语料库之间得到系统检验，因此本文旨在检验该假设的有效性。

Method: 使用Universal Dependencies v2.18中38对同语言树库，通过一致性相关系数、Bland-Altman分析和包含12种预处理规格的多宇宙设计，比较跨树库的平均依赖距离估计值，并检验树库替换对语言排序和组间方差的影响。

Result: 跨树库一致性仅为中等水平：替换树库导致约40%的语言对排序逆转，树库选择解释约29%的组间方差。这种分歧远超树库内采样误差，且在全部12种预处理规格下持续存在。然而，所有树库均证实依赖长度最小化成立（归一化比率低于1）。

Conclusion: 平均依赖距离更应被视为受语法、语域和标注因素制约的语料条件复合指标，而非稳定的语言级参数。依赖长度最小化的定性普遍性在语料替换后依然成立，但跨语言的序数排序不具有稳健性。

Abstract: Dependency-distance estimates derived from a single corpus are routinely treated as properties of a language, yet this assumption has not been tested across independently compiled corpora. We compared mean dependency-distance estimates across 38 same-language treebank pairs from Universal Dependencies v2.18, using concordance correlation, Bland-Altman analysis, and a twelve-specification multiverse design. Cross-treebank agreement was moderate at best: substituting one treebank for another reversed nearly 40 percent of pairwise language orderings, and treebank choice accounted for roughly 29 percent of between-group variance. This disagreement substantially exceeded within-treebank sampling error and persisted across all twelve preprocessing specifications. Nevertheless, every treebank confirmed dependency-length minimization (normalized ratio below 1). The data are more consistent with MDD as a corpus-conditioned composite of grammatical, register, and annotation factors than as a stable language-level parameter: the qualitative DLM universal survives corpus substitution, but the ordinal cross-linguistic ranking does not.

</details>


### [83] [Memory as transformation: LETHE, a self-referential gan-inspired architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04289)
*Francesco Vitucci,Anthony Di Furia,Francesco Scagliola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LETHE是一个基于SuperCollider实现的自指涉声音系统，采用封闭的对抗生成网络架构，无需外部数据集。系统通过3×3混合矩阵处理音频，利用五特征线性判别器和类似REINFORCE的随机扰动优化器共同演化九个系数和两个延迟时间参数，15次消融实验均证明主动生成器对参数演化不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自指涉电子音乐传统中，如何将声音结果委派给自适应闭环系统，使作曲家的角色定位为参数空间的定义者而非直接的声音控制者。

Method: 在SuperCollider中构建封闭式GAN架构：音频经3×3混合矩阵（含两条延迟线）处理，九个系数和两个延迟时间由五特征线性判别器与单样本REINFORCE式随机扰动优化器的交互驱动演化，判别器将当前能量行为与初始状态存档比较以引导参数更新，圆形、固定及实时声源可独立混合。

Result: 固定和圆形会话中加入消融对照实验，15次消融会话中参数变化Δc22均为0.000，证明主动生成器是参数演化的必要条件。

Conclusion: 该工作定位于自指涉电子音乐传统，验证了自适应闭环系统能够独立驱动参数演化，将作曲建构成参数空间的定义行为，而非直接操控声音的生成过程。

Abstract: LETHE (Latent-parameter Evolution with Temporal Hierarchical quasi-Equilibrium) is a self-referential sonic-oblivion system implemented in SuperCollider. It adopts the formal vocabulary of Generative Adversarial Networks in a closed configuration without external datasets or supervision after initialization. Audio is processed by a 3 x 3 mixing matrix built around two delay lines; its nine coefficients and two delay times evolve through the interaction of a five-feature linear discriminator and a random-perturbation optimizer analogous to single-sample REINFORCE. The discriminator compares current energy behavior with an archive of the initial state and guides parameter updates. Circular, fixed, and live sources can be mixed independently. Across fixed and circular sessions with an ablation control, the active generator is necessary for parametric evolution ($Δc_{22}=0.000$ in all 15 ablation sessions). Situated in the tradition of self-referential electroacoustic music, LETHE delegates the sonic outcome to an adaptive closed loop whose parametric space is defined by the composer.

</details>


### [84] [Evidence Integration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04290)
*Sebastien Kawada,Manolis Kellis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分布性理论解释LLM如何整合外部证据（工具检索、RAG等），指出证据整合受接收者先验权重和候选证据倾斜驱动，产生三个可预测现象：更可能的候选更具说服力、接收者更易整合自身特征性错误、相同证据可能提升弱模型但损害强模型。通过千万级试验、12个LLM和8个领域验证，并揭示该机制是接收者特定的控制策略而非对证据源的标量信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM越来越多地依赖外部证据进行推理，但LLM如何将这些证据整合进其已开始形成的决策中仍不清楚。作者希望从理论上解释这一证据整合机制的底层分布性规律。

Method: 提出分布性理论框架，其中证据通过接收者先验权重和候选证据倾斜来转移接收者的初始答案分布。通过超过千万次试验，使用4个系列的12个LLM，在8个领域（包括量子力学、物理学、遗传学、分子生物学等4个科学发现任务）中进行验证。还采用因果干预实验和J-lens分解分析网络内部机制。

Result: 三个预测均得到验证。LLM甚至在内验证候选无效后仍会整合证据（命题约束下93-100%，物理生命科学推理中最高99.4%）。因果干预显示候选整合在网络后期以结构化序列步骤实现，而验证的表征虽可解码但对答案几乎没有因果影响。J-lens分解表明语言化验证的底层状态与候选整合的底层状态完全可分离。

Conclusion: 证据整合是接收者特有的控制策略而非对证据源的标量信任，由接收者属性决定。研究还提出接收者相关的可靠性边界：接收者一致的错误比同频率的随机错误更能压低性能，为理解LLM证据推理的可靠性和可解释性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Despite increasing reliance on LLMs that reason with external evidence supplied by tools, retrieval-augmented generation, other agents, and users, how LLMs integrate such evidence into decisions they have already begun to form remains largely unclear. We present a distributional theory in which evidence shifts the receiver's distribution of initial answers, driven by a receiver prior weight and a candidate evidence tilt, leading to three predictions. First, candidates more probable to the receiver are more persuasive. Second, receivers more readily integrate characteristic errors of their own than foreign errors from different sources. Third, identical evidence can improve weaker models and harm stronger ones. We confirm these over ten million trials, twelve LLMs from four families, and eight domains, four of them scientific discovery tasks in the physical and life sciences: quantum mechanics, physics, genetics, and molecular biology. The law also yields a receiver-relative reliability frontier: receiver-congruent errors depress performance more steeply than random errors of the same rate. LLMs also integrate candidates even after internally verifying their invalidity (93-100% with propositional constraints; up to 99.4% on held-out physical and life-sciences reasoning), demonstrating evidence integration is a receiver-specific control policy over existing distributions, determined by receiver properties rather than scalar trust in the evidence source. Causal interventions show candidate integration is implemented late in the network, as a structured sequence of steps admitting external candidate answers, promoting them, and transporting them into the answer state. Representations of verification are decodable but have little causal impact on answers. A J-lens decomposition shows the state underlying verbalized verification is fully dissociable from that underlying candidate integration.

</details>


### [85] [MedProb: Probing Internal Representations of Vision-Language Models for Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04336)
*Erfan Nourbakhsh,Ke Yang,Anthony Rios*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MedProb轻量级探测框架，无需医疗微调、大模型或多智能体管线，仅通过冻结VLM表征的线性探针即可在多选题医学视觉问答中超越提示方法、医疗VLM和智能体系统。研究还发现较小VLM中包含更多可恢复的Med-VQA信号，且医疗适配并非持续改善线性可解码性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视医学视觉问答（Med-VQA）研究中的普遍假设——即需要医学微调、大型模型或复杂多智能体管线才能取得好性能。作者希望通过轻量探测方法验证：冻结VLM表征中是否已蕴含大量可恢复的答案相关信号，而不必依赖生成式评估。

Method: MedProb——轻量级探测框架，从冻结的VLM表征中直接预测多选题Med-VQA答案，不进行自由文本生成。在PATH-VQA、SLAKE和VQA-RAD三个数据集上评测，并通过与医学VLM配对对比14组实验分析线性可解码性；另用拒绝采样评分程序扩展至开放式生成设置。

Result: MedProb比提示方法恢复更多答案相关信号，性能优于医学VLM和智能体系统；与提示相比，探测缩小了小模型与大模型之间的差距；14组对比中医学适配并未一致提升线性可解码性；自由文本生成本身存在高达10个百分点的答案位置偏差，而MedProb虽也有位置偏差但影响方式不同。

Conclusion: Med-VQA的主要瓶颈可能不在模型参数规模或领域微调，而在于生成式评估方式未能充分挖掘冻结VLM中已有的答案信号。该工作为主流假设提供了反例，并提出更简单、更有效地评估和利用多模态预训练模型的新思路。

Abstract: Medical visual question answering (Med-VQA) is often assumed to require medical fine-tuning, large models, or complex multi-agent pipelines. We revisit this assumption with \textbf{MedProb}, a lightweight probing framework that predicts multiple-choice Med-VQA answers from frozen VLM representations without free-text generation. Across PATH-VQA, SLAKE, and VQA-RAD, MedProb recovers substantially more answer-relevant signal than prompting and performs stronger than medical VLMs and agentic systems. Probing also reduces the apparent gap between small and large models compared to prompting, suggesting that smaller VLMs contain more recoverable Med-VQA signal than generation-based evaluation reveals. Across 14 matched general-purpose and medical VLM pairs, medical adaptation does not consistently improve this linear decodability. Finally, free-text generation exhibits an answer-position bias of up to 10 percentage points, whereas MedProb also has positional bias, however, it is impacted differently than prompting. Our main results target the multiple-choice/multiclass Med-VQA setting; we additionally show the probe can be extended to open-ended generation via a rejection-sampling scoring procedure.

</details>


### [86] [Adapting from Downturns: Prediction of Long-Term Conversational-Skill Development in Mental-Health Crisis Counselors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04350)
*Vivian Nguyen,Lillian Lee,Elizabeth A. Olson,Cristian Danescu-Niculescu-Mizil*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项新任务：在心理咨询师的职业生涯早期预测其是否能最终改善引导对话走向积极结果的能力。核心洞见在于，通过识别咨询师最初遇到的困难时刻类型、观察他们在后续对话中如何适应这些时刻，可以预测其数月甚至数年后的改善情况。实验表明，该方法优于直接从会话文本中学习的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 志愿者心理咨询师的对话技能至关重要，但他们往往缺乏监督和结构化反馈。理解咨询师如何发展引导对话产生积极结果的能力，并尽早识别哪些咨询师（不）在进步轨迹上，有助于优先为最需要支持的咨询师提供帮助。

Method: 设计了一个方法框架：首先识别咨询师最初难以应对的时刻类型；然后捕捉他们在后续对话中再次遇到相似时刻时如何调整回应；最后学习哪些早期调整能够预测数月甚至数年后的改善。

Result: 尽管这项未来预测任务具有挑战性，但提出的"咨询师适应"方法在预测效果上优于直接从对话转录中学习的基线方法，证明了该任务的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过关注咨询师在困难时刻的适应模式而非泛泛的对话内容，可以有效地早期预测咨询师的长期成长轨迹，从而为培训和支持资源的分配提供更有针对性的依据。

Abstract: How do people learn to become better conversationalists? This question is especially important in the context of mental-health counseling, where conversational skills are essential, yet volunteer counselors often have limited access to supervision and structured feedback. Understanding how counselors develop their ability to steer conversations toward positive outcomes -- and identifying early which counselors are (not) on track to improve -- can help prioritize support for the counselors who need it most.
  In this work, we introduce the task of predicting, early in a conversationalist's career, whether they will eventually improve at steering conversations toward positive outcomes, and demonstrate the feasibility of this task in the case of volunteer mental-health crisis counselors. Our central insight is that people may struggle with particular kinds of moments in a conversation, and that what is especially revealing of their likelihood of future improvement is how they learn to handle those moments over time. We operationalize this insight by designing a method that identifies the types of moments a counselor initially struggles with, captures how they adapt their response when they re-encounter similar moments in subsequent conversations, and learns which early adaptations predict improvement months or even years later. While this future-prediction task is challenging, our counselor-adaptation approach yields better results than baselines that learn directly from the conversation transcript.

</details>


### [87] [VERGE: Verification-Enhanced Refinement for Grounded Extraction of Early-Onset Colorectal Cancer Symptoms in Clinical Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04366)
*Nikkie Hooman,Monarch Nigam,Amy E. Hughes,Rasmi G. Nair,Mehak Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了VERGE，一种基于检索增强生成与有界验证-修正循环的代理工作流，用于从自由文本临床记录中自动提取早发性结直肠癌的六种红色警示症状和家族史风险状态。在4,033对临床标注数据上，相比单代理基线，VERGE将精确率从0.764提升至0.849，MCC从0.681提升至0.730，且仅1.5%的声明需要人工审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早发性结直肠癌在年轻成年人中发病率上升，但该年龄段缺乏基于证据的红色警示症状随访检测指南；结构化电子病历数据无法捕捉症状持续时间、背景和家族史等关键细节，亟需从非结构化临床文本中自动化提取可靠信息以支持早期检测。

Method: VERGE采用代理工作流：首先通过检索增强生成生成初步标签与证据，然后进入有界的验证-修正循环，该循环检查文本依据和临床有效性，对声明进行纠错和复检直至解决或达到限制，未解决的声明升级至人工审核。评估在4,033对临床医生标注的笔记发现对上开展，并与单代理基线、基于规则的临床语言处理基线和替代底层语言模型进行对比。

Result: 相比单代理基线，VERGE减少了假阳性发现，精确率从0.764提升至0.849，MCC从0.681提升至0.730，在精确率-召回率权衡上获得均衡增益；大部分标记错误被自主解决，仅1.5%的声明需要人工审核。

Conclusion: 有界的、基于验证的工作流能够在保证真阳性检测能力的同时减少不必要的阳性发现，为构建更可靠、更值得信赖的临床语言处理工具以支持年轻患者结直肠癌风险评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Early-onset colorectal cancer is increasing among younger adults, yet red-flag symptoms in this age group have no evidence-based guidelines for follow-up testing, and structured encounter data do not capture the detail needed to support early detection and inform follow-up, including symptom duration, context, and fam- ily history, an established colorectal-cancer risk factor. This study aimed to develop and evaluate an automated method for extracting six red-flag symptoms and family-history risk status from free-text clinical notes. We developed VERGE, an agentic workflow in which an initial label and evidence are proposed using retrieval-augmented generation, then passed through a bounded verification- refinement cycle that checks textual grounding and clinical validity, corrects and rechecks a claim until resolved or a limit is reached, and escalates unresolved claims for human review. VERGE was evaluated on 4,033 clinician-labeled note-finding pairs against a single-agent baseline, a rule-based clinical language-processing baseline, and an alternative underlying language model. Compared with the single-agent baseline, VERGE reduced false positive find- ings, improving precision from 0.764 to 0.849 and MCC from 0.681 to 0.730, a balanced gain across the precision-recall trade-off, and resolved most flagged errors autonomously, with human review required for only 1.5 percent of claims. These results indicate that a bounded, verification-based workflow can reduce unnecessary positive findings without sacrificing the ability to detect true ones. This approach offers a path toward more reliable and trustworthy clinical language-processing tools to support colorectal cancer risk assessment in younger patients.

</details>


### [88] [You Really Didn't Get That? Benchmarking Social Pragmatic Inference for Indirect and Playful Chinese Online Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04384)
*Shiwei Hong,Junjie Ma,Emma Jiren Wang,Ethan Z. Rong,Siying Hu,Haichang Li,Ziying Wang,Zhicong Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基准测试，用于评估大语言模型能否理解中文社交媒体评论中依赖情境的间接、诙谐语用含义。基于20多万条公开中文社交互动记录，构建了4,735个人工验证的诊断条目，每项将目标评论与前文语境和可能的误读配对。最强的模型仅达到81.42%的leave-writer-out准确率，而人类准确率为90.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估大多围绕预定义现象或受控语用类别组织，无法判断模型是否能区分自然发生的评论在特定交流中所传达的合理解读，因此需要一个新的基准来评估模型恢复这种情境化语用含义的能力。

Method: 从20多万条公开中文社交媒体互动记录中构建了4,735个人工验证的诊断条目，每条将目标评论与重构的前文语境及合理误读配对。在交叉作者（cross-writer）设置下，评估了八个LLM分别作为问题编写者和解答者的表现。

Result: 最强模型leave-writer-out准确率为81.42%，八个模型平均为68.70%，而人类准确率为90.8%。案例分析显示，模型常能识别出宽泛的反讽或嬉戏语气，但在识别具体机制或交互行为时会出错。

Conclusion: 该任务对LLM而言具有挑战性，与人类表现存在显著差距；模型在把握宽泛的语用语气方面尚可，但在细粒度地理解言语行为的机制和互动意图方面仍有明显不足，为未来研究指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Chinese online comments often convey social meaning through indirect and playful language that is hard to interpret without context. Existing evaluations largely organize items around predefined phenomena or controlled pragmatic categories, leaving open whether models can distinguish plausible readings of what a naturally occurring comment is doing in a particular exchange. We introduce a benchmark for evaluating whether LLMs can recover such situated pragmatic meanings. From more than 200,000 public Chinese social media interaction records, we construct 4,735 human-validated diagnostic items, each pairing a target comment with reconstructed preceding context and plausible misreadings. We evaluate eight LLMs as both question writers and solvers in a cross-writer setting. The task is challenging: the strongest model achieves 81.42% leave-writer-out accuracy. Across all eight models, the mean leave-writer-out accuracy is 68.70% while human accuracy was 90.8%. Case analysis shows that models often recognize broad irony or playfulness while misidentifying the mechanism or interactional move.

</details>


### [89] [Evaluation of Phonetic Encoding Algorithms on Transcription Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04391)
*Can Özbey,Emre Kaplan,Berkin Deniz Kahya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Hüllermeier-Rifqi指数（广义Rand Index变体）的新颖评估方案，用于衡量语音编码算法与IPA（国际音标）转录的一致性。通过计算真实转录与语音编码之间归一化编辑距离的绝对差异得到不一致分数，并依据随机字符串生成器进行校正。研究在多语言转录数据集上评估了多种语音编码器的性能及其碰撞率召回能力，并展示了该方法在测量语言书写系统正字法透明度方面的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音编码算法的评估方法缺乏统一且严谨的框架，难以衡量编码结果与IPA标准转录之间的实际一致性程度，需要一种能全面评估编码器性能的方案。

Method: 基于Hüllermeier-Rifqi指数构建评估方案；使用归一化编辑距离作为置换依赖的字符串度量计算成对相似度；通过真实转录与编码结果的绝对差异获得不一致分数；用同字母表随机字符串生成器进行分数校正；在多语言转录数据集上评估多种语音编码器。

Result: 在多种语音编码器和多语言数据集上完成了系统评估，同时基于碰撞率分析了编码器的召回能力；验证了该方案可进一步应用于衡量语言的正字法透明度。

Conclusion: 所提出的评估方案不仅能为语音编码算法提供有效的质量评估手段，还能拓展到语言正字法透明度的测量，具有广泛的理论与实际应用价值。

Abstract: In this work, a novel evaluation scheme built on a generalized variant of the Rand Index measure, namely, the Hüllermeier-Rifqi Index, is proposed in order to assess how well phonetic encoding algorithms conform to word-based transcriptions in IPA (International Phonetic Alphabet) notation. For this objective, the discordance score is obtained by calculating the absolute difference between the pairwise similarity values of ground-truth transcriptions and those of corresponding phonetic encodings, which are computed using normalized edit distance as a permutation dependent string metric. The resulting score is subsequently adjusted with respect to that of a random string generator incorporating the same alphabet as the encoder under consideration. A wide range of phonetic encoders were evaluated as such on multi-lingual transcription datasets along with their recall capabilities based on the collision rate. The validity of the proposed scheme is further supported by its applicability in measuring the orthographic transparency of a language when the writing system is viewed as an inherent phonetic representation.

</details>


### [90] [The Anatomy of an ASR Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04404)
*Hamees Sayed,Apoorv Singh,Kumar Aman,Akshat Mandloi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了ASR系统中的"幻觉"（生成与语音无关的流畅文本）现象，将其视为更广泛的"接地失败"（transcript不再受音频引导）的一种后果。作者通过研究两个独立训练的Conformer-Large识别器（CTC和RNN-T），在环境退化与说话人背景偏移条件下发现：最终编码器阶段是关键的边界——绕过最终块导致几乎每个话语都发散，而绕过中间块影响甚微。在相同阶段，表征变得更紧凑、文本可被解码器读取、字形信息变得显式。然而干预产生的是杂乱或重复输出而非流畅的伪造文本。因此该结果识别的是幻觉的"机制性前提"（无法产生充分接地输出），而非自然发生幻觉的完整起源。研究揭示了两种解码器家族和多种分布偏移下一致的终端阶段依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解ASR系统为何会生成与输入语音无关但看似流畅的文本（幻觉），并将其作为接地失效的一种表现来研究，定位该失效在模型中何处变得可能。

Method: 对CTC和RNN-T两种独立训练的Conformer-Large识别器进行系统性的编码器块绕过（bypass）干预实验，并在环境退化和说话人背景偏移等分布变化条件下分析各编码器阶段对识别行为的贡献。

Result: 两个模型中最末编码器块是关键边界：绕过它导致几乎所有话语输出发散；而绕过中间块影响很小。在同一阶段，表征更紧凑、文本对训练解码器可读、字形信息显式化。但干预导致的输出是杂乱/重复而非流畅伪造。

Conclusion: 最终编码器阶段是接地识别的关键依赖点，两个解码器家族（CTC与RNN-T）在不同分布偏移下表现出一致性。该工作识别出幻觉产生的机制性前提——无法生成充分接地的输出——但流畅幻觉的完整起源仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: ASR systems sometimes produce fluent text that is unrelated to the speech they receive. We view these hallucinations as one possible consequence of a broader grounding failure, in which the transcript is no longer adequately guided by the audio. To understand where this failure becomes possible, we study two independently trained Conformer-Large recognizers - one CTC and one RNN-T - under environmental degradation and speaker-background shift. In both models, the final encoder stage emerges as a critical boundary: bypassing the final block causes divergence on nearly every utterance, whereas bypassing middle blocks has little effect. At this same stage, the representations become more compact, text becomes readable by the trained decoder, and grapheme information becomes explicit. Importantly, the intervention produces garbled or repetitive output rather than fluent fabrication. Our result therefore identifies a mechanistic precondition for hallucination - the failure to produce adequately grounded output - not the complete origin of naturally occurring hallucinations. Together, the results reveal a consistent terminal-stage dependency for grounded recognition across two decoder families and multiple distribution shifts.

</details>


### [91] [A Systematic Evaluation of Cross-Lingual Consistency Enhancement Methods in Multilingual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04409)
*Jirui Qi,Mingyang Wang,Hinrich Schütze,Raquel Fernández,Arianna Bisazza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对提升多语言模型跨语言一致性（CLC）的方法进行了统一评估，涵盖推理时干预和后训练方法，跨越三个模型家族和三个封闭式基准。研究发现后训练方法整体更可靠，直接分布对齐在所有组合中均能提升CLC，而其他方法对答案格式和语言覆盖面更敏感。此外，跨领域迁移在源任务和目标任务输出格式相似时才有效。论文还探讨了CLC增强是否损害模型在文化相关问题上的差异化回答能力，发现封闭式评估无系统性退化，但开放生成中偶尔出现准确率下降，尤其对非英语回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨语言一致性（CLC）增强方法在不同研究中使用了不同的模型、任务和评估协议，导致各种方法的相对优劣不明确，缺乏统一、可比的系统评估。

Method: 构建统一的评估框架，对代表性的CLC增强方法（涵盖推理时干预和后训练方法）在三个模型家族和三个封闭式问答基准上进行系统比较；同时引入两个文化多样性问答基准，检验CLC增强是否影响模型对文化依赖问题的差异化回答能力，并分别考察封闭式评估和开放式生成两种协议。

Result: 后训练方法整体更可靠，直接分布对齐在所有模型-数据集组合中均能一致提升CLC；跨领域迁移仅在源任务与目标任务输出格式相似时有效；CLC增强在封闭式文化问答评估中无系统性退化，但开放式生成中偶发准确率下降，尤其影响非英语回答。

Conclusion: CLC增强需要在跨领域鲁棒性和文化适当变异两方面同时进行评估，直接分布对齐是当前最稳健的后训练策略，未来工作应关注后训练方法的改进和文化差异化问答基准的开发。

Abstract: Multilingual language models often produce inconsistent answers to semantically equivalent questions across languages, motivating methods to improve cross-lingual consistency (CLC). However, existing methods are typically evaluated using different models, tasks, and protocols, leaving their relative strengths unclear. In this work, we present a unified evaluation of representative CLC-enhancement methods for question answering, spanning inference-time interventions and post-training approaches across three model families and three closed-form benchmarks. The results show that post-training methods are generally more reliable, with direct distribution alignment consistently improving CLC across all model-dataset combinations, while other methods are more sensitive to answer format and the breadth of language coverage. Notably, cross-domain transfer is limited unless source and target tasks share similar output formats. We further investigate whether CLC enhancement hurts models' ability to respond differently *when needed*, that is, when asked culture-dependent questions. Across two benchmarks of culturally diverse question answering, we find no systematic degradation in controlled closed-form evaluation, whereas open-ended generation reveals occasional accuracy reductions, particularly for non-English responses. Our work highlights the need to evaluate CLC enhancement for both cross-domain robustness and culturally appropriate variation, informing future work in post-training and benchmark development.

</details>


### [92] [What Attention Recalls and Recurrence Controls in Hybrid Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04434)
*Kirill Afendulev,Alexey Dontsov,Elena Tutubalina,Anton Korznikov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过两种缓存级干预方法（Split-prefill和State-swap）系统研究了混合语言模型中注意力机制与固定大小循环状态各自的功能角色。研究发现两种通道的功能分工极为明确：精确检索（exact retrieval）完全依赖注意力机制，而输出语言风格和人格特征则主要由循环状态控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合语言模型将注意力机制与固定大小的循环状态相结合，但这两条通道各自承担什么功能、如何分工协作尚不清晰。理解这一分工对于解释和进一步改进混合架构模型至关重要。

Method: 提出两种缓存级干预方法：(1) Split-prefill——仅保留KV缓存或仅保留循环状态中的一种来生成答案；(2) State-swap——将来自一个上下文的KV缓存与来自另一个上下文的循环状态配对，在单次前向传播中生成。实验在Qwen3.5和Falcon-H1两个模型上进行。

Result: 两个通道呈截然相反的功能分工：精确检索仅通过注意力存活（保持完整准确率的64-98%），而通过循环状态则完全降至零。输出语言与人格特质则相反——循环状态可保留70-80%（语言）和3-5倍（人格）的表现，而仅保留KV缓存时语言准确率跌至约1%。State-swap因果验证表明：答案的内容来自KV侧，而语言来自循环状态侧。

Conclusion: 注意力机制提供了对"说了什么"的精确查表功能，而循环状态则决定了模型"接下来如何表达"。两者在功能上形成清晰互补：注意力负责内容检索，循环状态负责语言风格与生成方式的塑造。此外，仅循环状态生成时还能接受上下文从未出现过但语义或构词相关的词汇，表明循环状态具备一定泛化能力。

Abstract: Hybrid language models combine attention with a fixed-size recurrent state, but the role of each channel remains unclear. We introduce two cache-level interventions. Split-prefill keeps only the KV cache or only the recurrent state from a prefilled context, then generates an answer. State-swap pairs the KV cache from one context with the recurrent state from another in a single forward pass. On Qwen3.5 and Falcon-H1, the two channels split sharply by function. Exact retrieval survives only through attention (64-98% of full accuracy) and collapses to zero through recurrence. Output language and persona reverse the pattern: both survive recurrence (70-80% and 3-5x) while KV-only drops to ~1% language accuracy. State-swap confirms this causally: the answer takes its value from the KV side and its language from the recurrent side. Recurrent-only generation also accepts words that were never in the context but share meaning or parts with seen items. Attention provides a lookup over what was said; the recurrent state shapes how the model says it next.

</details>


### [93] [GRACE: Graph-Grounded Reflective Agent Copilot Engine for Expert-in-the-Loop Knowledge Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04442)
*John Seon Keun Yi,Joshua R. Minot,Dokyun Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GRACE是一个基于图结构的知识验证框架，将LLM生成的回复分解为原子声明，在加权二部图中与可信知识先验进行对照，通过加权中心性分析将声明分类为Grounded、Refuted或Boundary，并结合专家在环验证机制和"注意力回报"(RoA)目标来高效分配审阅资源，从而系统级缓解幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险场景下部署的LLM经常生成看似合理但缺乏依据的内容。标准RAG流程只能检索孤立的文本片段，无法追踪跨文档的证据关系或量化不确定性，导致对幻觉的缓解能力有限。需要一种更系统化的方法，不仅识别幻觉，还能处理模型知识边界上的新颖或有争议的声明。

Method: GRACE将LLM响应解构为原子声明，并在加权二部图中对照可信知识先验进行grounding。通过边权重编码声明与先验的接近程度，利用加权中心性分析将声明分类为Grounded、Refuted或Boundary。同时提出Return on Attention (RoA)目标函数，仅在声明的优先级加权不确定性超过验证成本时才将其推迟到专家审查。专家验证后的声明被提升为新的证据锚点，形成闭环的知识库扩展迭代机制。

Result: 在多个语言模型及多个通用和特定领域知识数据集上的评估中，GRACE的知识库作为检索基础的可靠性优于RAG基线，RoA框架能高效选择有价值的边界知识进行专家验证，验证了图结构表示结合专家在环验证框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 图结构化表示与专家在环验证相结合，可以在系统层面而非生成层面有效缓解幻觉问题，为高风险的LLM部署提供了一种更可靠的知识grounding方案。

Abstract: Large language models deployed in high-stakes settings frequently generate plausible but ungrounded claims. Standard retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines offer limited remedy, since they retrieve isolated passages without tracking cross-document evidence relationships or quantifying uncertainty. We introduce GRACE (Graph-grounded Reflective Agent Copilot Engine), a framework that deconstructs LLM responses into atomic claims and grounds them against trusted knowledge priors within a weighted bipartite graph. Edge weights encode the closeness of each claim to the priors, enabling weighted centrality analysis that classifies claims as Grounded, Refuted, or Boundary. Such classification identifies not just hallucinations but also novel or contested claims at the frontier of the model's knowledge. To efficiently allocate human or agent resources, we formulate a Return on Attention (RoA) objective that defers a claim to expert review only when its priority-weighted uncertainty exceeds the cost of verification. Claims verified by experts are promoted to new evidence anchors, closing a validator-LLM evolutionary loop that expands the knowledge base across iterations. We evaluate GRACE across multiple language models and on datasets spanning both general and domain-specific knowledge. Our results show that our knowledge base serves as a reliable foundation for retrieval that outperforms RAG baselines, and that the RoA framework efficiently selects valuable boundary knowledge for expert verification. These findings demonstrate that graph-structured representations combined with expert-in-the-loop verification can mitigate hallucination at the system level rather than at the generation level. Code available at https://github.com/johnsk95/grace_code

</details>


### [94] [TRILOGUE: A Trilingual Spoken Dialogue Fact-Checking Benchmark with Evidence and Paired Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04452)
*Chaewan Chun,Meruyert Aristombayeva,Jiyoung Choi,Mahjabin Nahar,Delvin Ce Zhang,Dongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TRILOGUE，一个大规模三语（英语、俄语、哈萨克语）口语对话事实核查基准，包含近1.2万个对话、18.7万个轮次及390小时配音频数据，支持声明可核查性检测、证据检索和声明验证三项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代错误信息常通过语音传播，但现有事实核查系统主要基于书面文本评估；已有的口语对话事实核查资源规模小、仅限英语或只关注标注而非端到端基准测试，缺乏大规模多语言基准。

Method: 构建TRILOGUE多语言口语对话基准：涵盖英、俄、哈三种语言，包含ASR转录和词级时间戳对齐，约5000个人类录制的俄语和哈萨克语对话文件；支持声明可核查性检测、源文章证据检索和声明验证三个任务，提供claim-only、gold-evidence和retrieved-evidence三种输入设置。

Result: 基线实验表明ASR退化与跨语言迁移仍具挑战性，尤其对哈萨克语而言；使用检索到的源证据可大幅缩小与gold-evidence验证之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: TRILOGUE填补了大规模多语言口语对话事实核查基准的空白，为端到端基准测试提供了评测平台，但仍需应对ASR噪声和低资源语言（如哈萨克语）的挑战，检索增强是缓解验证困难的有效途径。

Abstract: Modern misinformation is often heard before it is read, yet fact-checking systems are still evaluated mainly on clean written claims. Spoken dialogue remains different even when systems operate on transcripts: claims may be distributed across speakers and turns, depend on prior context, and become harder to verify when Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) errors distort the available text. Prior spoken dialogue fact-checking resources are small, English-centric, or focused on annotation rather than end-to-end benchmarking, leaving no large multilingual benchmark with paired speech and turn-level labels. We introduce TRILOGUE (TRIlingual spoken diaLOGUE fact-checking), a large-scale trilingual benchmark of source-grounded spoken dialogues in English, Russian, and Kazakh. It contains nearly 12K dialogues, 187K turns, and 390 hours of paired audio with ASR transcripts and word-level timestamp alignments across all three languages, including nearly 5K human-recorded Russian and Kazakh dialogue files. TRILOGUE supports claim check-worthiness detection, source-article evidence retrieval, and claim verification with claim-only, gold-evidence, and retrieved-evidence inputs. Baselines show that ASR degradation and cross-lingual transfer remain challenging, especially for Kazakh, while retrieved source evidence substantially narrows the gap to gold-evidence verification.

</details>


### [95] [Shared circuits predict whether LLMs generalize across formats in arithmetic reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04463)
*Andrea Gregor de Varda,Sana Pandey,Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在算术推理中跨格式（数字格式vs语言格式）泛化能否从模型内部机制预测。通过归因路径分析定位模型解决数字算术（如2+5）与文字算术（如two plus five）的电路，发现数字电路与语言电路的重叠程度能预测模型对三种语言（英、西、意）文字格式的泛化表现，其预测能力与监督探针相当且无需标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能轻松在不同输入格式间泛化（如2+5和two plus five），但LLM对表面格式变化更脆弱。作者希望探究能否从模型内部机制预测跨格式泛化能力，从而深入理解模型行为和局限。

Method: 使用归因路径分析（attribution patching）分别定位模型解决数字算术和文字算术（三种语言）时的神经电路；然后测试数字电路与各语言文字电路的重叠程度是否预测模型的泛化表现。

Result: 电路重叠在三个层面均能预测泛化：解释了三种文字格式的相对难度差异，预测了模型泛化最好的格式，也预测了个体题目的正确与否。性能与监督探针相当，但不需要任何标注数据。

Conclusion: 模型内部电路的共享程度是跨格式泛化能力的有力预测指标，说明LLM在算术推理中的格式偏差可追溯至其内部神经机制的差异，为理解和预测模型泛化行为提供了新的无监督视角。

Abstract: In many forms of reasoning, including arithmetic reasoning, generalizing across superficial changes in input format is effortless for humans: anyone who can solve 2+5 can also solve 'two plus five'. In contrast, LLMs are more brittle to surface variations of the prompts: for example, they solve numeric arithmetic problems almost perfectly but are substantially less accurate on verbal renditions of the same problems. Here, we ask whether generalization across formats can be predicted from the models' internals. Using attribution patching, we first independently localize the circuit that each model recruits to solve numeric arithmetic problems (2+5) vs. verbal ones, in three languages: English ('two plus five'), Spanish ('dos más cinco'), and Italian ('due più cinque'); then, we test whether overlap with the model's own numeric circuit predicts its generalization to the verbal formats. Indeed, we find support for this idea at three levels: circuit overlap accounts for the relative difficulty of the three verbal formats, for which models generalize best, and for which items are solved correctly, rivaling supervised probes while requiring no labeled data.

</details>


### [96] [Safety for Whom? Boundary-Aware Self-Distillation for Controlled LLM Safety Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04482)
*Alejo López-Ávila,Iker García-Ferrero,Jezabel Garcia,Antonio Tiene,Román Orús*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"窄边界安全"（Narrow-Boundary Safety）框架，通过离线自生成数据方法（受控主题生成、覆盖修复、分布内补偿数据、有害-良性配对）来解决同主题下区分"应拒绝"与"可回答"内容的安全对齐问题。实验表明该方法能显著提升目标域拒绝率并降低有害响应率，但过度拒绝率随之上升，需通过数据组成来平衡安全与可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全对齐在"主题层面"判定内容是否有害，但实际部署需要更细粒度的"窄边界"——例如同样涉及选举话题，政治操纵应被拒绝，而事实性问题应被回答。现有方法难以在相同主题内区分这两种情况。

Method: 提出离线自生成框架，包含四个核心组件：受控主题生成（controlled topic generation）确保覆盖目标场景、覆盖修复（coverage repair）补齐遗漏场景、分布内补偿数据（in-distribution compensation data）保证数据质量、以及有害-良性配对数据（harmful-benign pairs）用于训练和评估。此外引入Escalate机制（逐步重试）完成拒绝痕迹的生成。

Result: 单次生成有19.88%的提示缺乏拒绝痕迹，而逐步重试仅剩0.20%。在政治说服场景下（Qwen3-8B），目标域拒绝率从9.47%提升至84.75%，三个更广泛的有害性基准中平均不安全响应率从26.26%降至0.14%，但XSTest的过度拒绝率从2.00%升至74.00%。用验证过的目标模型响应替代外部响应可将过度拒绝从15.20%降至5.20%。边界配对数据使保留对的合规侧过度拒绝从32.94%降至4.16%，而有害侧拒绝率仅从91.88%微降至87.72%。

Conclusion: 数据组成方式直接决定了安全性与可用性之间的权衡，安全对齐应在预期拒绝边界的两侧（有害侧和合规侧）同时进行评估，而非仅在单一维度上衡量。

Abstract: Safety alignment is usually posed as a topic-level question: is this subject harmful? Deployments ask a narrower one. A civics tutor and a public-sector assistant may share a base model yet need different boundaries inside the same topic, refusing targeted political manipulation while still answering factual questions about the same election. We formulate this as narrow-boundary safety and introduce an offline self-generated framework combining controlled topic generation, coverage repair, in-distribution compensation data, and harmful-benign pairs for training and evaluation. Single-shot generation leaves 19.88% of prompts without accepted refusal traces, whereas escalating retries leave 0.20%. On political persuasion with Qwen3-8B, training on refusal data completed through Escalate increases target-domain refusal from 9.47% to 84.75% and reduces the mean unsafe-response rate across three broader harmfulness benchmarks from 26.26% to 0.14%, but increases XSTest over-refusal from 2.00% to 74.00%. In a separate matched comparison, replacing external responses with verified target-model responses reduces over-refusal from 15.20% to 5.20%. Boundary-pair data reduces comply-side over-refusal on held-out pairs from 32.94% to 4.16%, while harmful-side refusal decreases only from 91.88% to 87.72%. These results show that data composition controls the safety and usability trade-off, and that safety alignment should be evaluated on both sides of the intended refusal boundary.

</details>


### [97] [Patterns of Priming in Production: Lexical, Semantic and Structural Alignment in Language Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04484)
*Giulia Pucci,Ruizhe Li,Arabella Sinclair*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型（LM）在句子生成中的结构启动效应，通过一系列受控的与格结构补全实验，发现LMs在语义连贯的句子中表现出显著的结构启动，且结构启动不仅受词汇语义连贯性增强，还会促进词汇语义层面的重复，表明结构启动跨越多个语言表征层面运作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作已在理解任务中证明了结构启动效应，但在生成（production）过程中，LM每一步需要从众多可能的延续中采样，结构启动效应是否依然存在尚不清楚。本文旨在探讨在有语义连贯性的自然句子生成中，前置结构语境如何影响后续句子的补全。

Method: 通过一系列受控的与格（dative）结构句子补全实验，系统比较了双宾语与介词宾语两种结构在不同条件（语义连贯性、基线对比）下的启动效应，分析了相对增量与绝对增量，并考察了词汇语义重复程度与结构启动之间的交互关系。

Result: 研究发现：（1）LMs确实表现出结构启动效应，尤其在语义连贯的句子中；（2）与基线相比，双宾语与格结构有更大的相对增长（符合逆频率效应），而更频繁产生的介词宾语结构有更大的绝对增长；（3）结构启动不仅被词汇语义连贯性增强，且被结构启动的补全也展现出更高程度的词汇语义重复。

Conclusion: 证据支持结构启动在LMs中跨越多个语言表征层面运作，句法、词汇与语义对齐之间相互促进，共同驱动LM生成中的结构启动现象。

Abstract: This paper investigates structural priming in language model (LM) production, examining how preceding structural context influences sentence completion. While prior work has demonstrated priming effects in comprehension of structural alternations, it remained unclear whether these persist in production, where, when generating, an LM samples from many possible continuations at each step. We address this question through a series of controlled sentence-completion experiments on dative constructions. In line with prior work, we find that LMs are susceptible to structural priming, particularly in sentences that are semantically coherent. In terms of priming magnitude, we find that while there is a greater relative increase of double-object datives against our baselines, in line with inverse frequency effects, there is a larger absolute increase in prepositional-objects, the more frequently produced construction. Finally, we not only observe that structural priming is boosted by lexico-semantic coherence, but that structurally primed completions display greater levels of lexico-semantic repetition. Taken together, our evidence supports the view that structural priming in LMs operates across multiple levels of linguistic representation, facilitating, and facilitated by syntactic, lexical, and semantic alignment. Code: https://github.com/the-context-lab/primedproduction.

</details>


### [98] [Cultural Misalignment in Large Language Models: Detection, Measurement, and Mitigation Through Targeted Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04485)
*Antoni Czolgowski,Abel Iyasele*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了三个开源LLM（Gemma3-12B、Bielik-11B-v3、Qwen3-4B）在63个人口统计学角色上的文化偏见，发现模型并未偏爱本国人口；通过LoRA微调可降低最差角色的偏见，但偏见会被重新分配而非消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM跨文化偏见研究多关注语言或国家层面的总体差异，缺乏对模型本国偏好假设的系统验证，也缺少针对最差表现人口角色的偏见缓解方法研究。

Method: 使用World Values Survey Wave 7数据构建63个人口统计学角色，选取三个国家的开源模型进行评估；用归一化Wasserstein距离量化分布偏差；对表现最差的五个角色进行LoRA微调（不到1200个训练样本、单GPU 15分钟内），并在国家层面进行偏差分解分析。

Result: 模型并未偏向本国人口，其中Qwen3-4B在中国人口上表现最差（W1=0.436）。LoRA微调使Bielik-11B偏差降低16.8%（p_Bonf=0.002, d=-4.4），五个目标角色均有改善，但最差角色从美国完全转换为中国老年人，说明偏差被重新分配而非移除。

Conclusion: 这是首项针对最差人口统计学角色进行LoRA微调以缓解跨文化偏见的研究；偏见缓解不能仅关注局部优化，需要更高层次的系统性方法。

Abstract: We evaluate three open-weight LLMs (Gemma3-12B from the USA, Bielik-11B-v3 from Poland, and Qwen3-4B from China) against World Values Survey Wave 7 data for 63 demographic personas across three countries, using normalized Wasserstein distance to quantify distributional misalignment. Contrary to expectations, no model favors its home country: the Chinese-built Qwen3-4B performs worst on its own Chinese population (W1 = 0.436, the highest misalignment in the entire model x country matrix). Targeted LoRA fine-tuning on the five worst-case personas, requiring fewer than 1,200 training pairs and under 15 minutes on a single GPU, reduces bias by 16.8% for Bielik-11B (p_Bonf = 0.002, d = -4.4) with all five targets improving. However, country-level decomposition reveals that fine-tuning redistributes rather than removes bias: Bielik's worst-case personas swap entirely from American to Chinese elderly, with zero overlap between pre- and post-correction sets. To our knowledge, this is the first study to target worst-case demographic personas with LoRA fine-tuning for cross-cultural bias mitigation.

</details>


### [99] [Towards Understanding Pause Token Fine-Tuning Dynamics: A Mode Retention Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04489)
*Jaehyeon Kim,Suhwan Kim,Nakyung Lee,Yeongoon Kim,Jimin Seo,Giho Lee,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从训练动力学视角重新审视暂停令牌（pause tokens）的作用，提出掩蔽边界暂停（MBP）方法——在推理步骤边界插入暂停令牌并掩蔽其损失。实验发现暂停令牌能够减少对先前学习分布的覆盖（保持旧模式），同时使边界邻接令牌编码更多下游步骤信息（非短视压缩）。在1B-8B的Qwen和Llama模型上，MBP在数学和代码推理任务上分别取得最高6分和2.5分的提升，且不影响通用语言能力，并将优势扩展到GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究将暂停令牌提升推理能力归因于计算表达力（computational expressivity），但对暂停令牌的训练动力学（training dynamics）缺乏深入探究。本文旨在填补这一空白，理解暂停令牌在微调过程中如何重塑训练动态，从而揭示其作用机制的另一种解释。

Method: 通过两个受控实验揭示两种不对称性：(H1) 在合成持续学习任务中，掩蔽暂停令牌在达到相同最终适应度时，对先前学习的分布覆盖减少约4倍（模式保持）；(H2) 在合成数学推理探测中，边界邻接令牌编码了显著更多的下游步骤信息（非短视压缩）。基于此形式化提出掩蔽边界暂停（MBP）训练规则：在推理步骤边界放置暂停令牌并掩蔽其损失。在1B-8B的Qwen和Llama模型上验证，并将该方法扩展到GRPO。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama系列1B-8B模型上，MBP一致地提升推理能力：数学任务最高提升6分，代码任务最高提升2.5分，同时保持通用语言理解能力不受损。该方法还成功将模式保持策略的优势扩展到GRPO强化学习训练中。

Conclusion: 本文重新定义了暂停令牌的本质角色：它不仅是推理时的计算设备，更是一种作用于保留-适应权衡（retention-adaptation trade-off）的训练动力学干预手段。这为理解暂停令牌的工作机制提供了新视角，并为未来设计更优的推理训练策略指明了方向。

Abstract: Pause-token methods improve LLM reasoning by inserting special tokens into sequences. Prior work explains these gains through computational expressivity. However, there is relatively little investigation into the training dynamics of pause tokens. We explore how pause tokens reshape the training dynamics of fine-tuning. Two controlled pilots expose distinct asymmetries. On a synthetic continual-learning task, masked pauses overwrite a previously-learned distribution roughly 4x less at matched final adaptation (H1, mode retention); on a synthetic math-reasoning probe, the boundary-adjacent token comes to encode substantially more downstream-step information (H2, non-myopic compression). We formalize a training rule consistent with both - Masked Boundary Pause (MBP), pause tokens placed at reasoning-step boundaries with their loss masked. Across 1B-8B Qwen and Llama models, MBP consistently improves reasoning, achieving gains of up to 6 points on math and 2.5 points on code, while preserving general language understanding abilities. We further demonstrate that this mode-preserving strategy extend gains to GRPO. These results recast pause tokens as a training-dynamics intervention on the retention-adaptation trade-off, rather than merely an inference-time computation device.

</details>


### [100] [LentEx: Generalizable Latent Entity Extraction via Synthetic Data and Instruction-Tuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04511)
*Umesh Bodhwani,Yuan Ling,Cibi Chakravarthy Senthilkumar,Shujing Dong,Yarong Feng,Hongfei Li,Ayush Goyal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍LentEx框架，利用合成数据生成和指令微调来优化小型高效大语言模型，用于解决潜在实体提取（LEE）任务，在MTEB聚类基准上超越现有最优模型，并展示了良好的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实体提取方法无法识别自由文本中隐含的、需要上下文推断的潜在实体，同时标记数据集的稀缺严重制约了该领域的发展。

Method: LentEx采用模板化方法生成多样化且上下文丰富的合成数据，并结合指令微调技术优化小型高效的大语言模型，以系统性地应对潜在实体提取挑战。

Result: LentEx在多项任务上均取得显著性能提升，特别是在MTEB聚类基准上超越了现有的最先进模型，并展现出对未见领域的强大泛化能力。

Conclusion: LentEx作为首个系统性通过大语言模型视角处理LEE的框架，为潜在实体理解与提取建立了新范式，可广泛应用于RAG和聚类等真实NLP任务中。

Abstract: Latent entity extraction (LEE) tackles the challenge of identifying implicit, contextually inferred entities within free text-an area where traditional entity extraction methods fall short. In this paper, we introduce LentEx, a novel framework for latent entity extraction that leverages synthetic data generation and instruction fine-tuning to optimize smaller, efficient large language models (LLMs). Latent entities, which are often abstract and thematic, are crucial for applications such as retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), customer persona analysis, and knowledge graph enrichment. LentEx addresses the scarcity of labeled datasets by employing a template-based approach to generate diverse, contextually rich synthetic data, ensuring high variability and alignment with real-world distributions. To our knowledge, LentEx is the first to systematically approach LEE through the lens of LLMs. LentEx demonstrates significant performance improvements across multiple tasks, notably surpassing state-of-the-art models on the MTEB Clustering Benchmark. Furthermore, our methodology enables robust generalization to unseen domains, making LentEx highly applicable in real-world NLP tasks, including RAG and clustering, thereby establishing a new paradigm for latent entity understanding and extraction in natural language processing.

</details>


### [101] [Scale-QLoRA: Code-Invariant Adapter Merging for Native 4-bit Microscaling LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04526)
*Tung-Ling Li,Jiale Huang,Lee-Chi Wang,Janaki Ram Gotei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Scale-QLoRA方法，在原生4位microscaling检查点（NVFP4/MXFP4）上仅适配per-block scale字段，冻结所有E2M1代码平面，使LoRA合并成为位精确恒等操作，避免传统合并后量化带来的精度损失（最高39个百分点）和产品生命周期中的代码耦合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA合并到基座模型是标准部署实践，但在原生4位microscaling检查点上合并不再免费——合并权重需通过量化器写回，重新推导离散E2M1代码平面，导致部署产物与特定量化约定耦合，且生命周期中任何触及代码的事件都会影响模型。朴素做法的量化器甚至可能删除适配效果（精度下降高达39个百分点），因为针对已在网格上的基座，重建的最优解就是基座本身。

Method: Scale-QLoRA的核心思想是：仅适配原生per-block scale字段，在该格式的部署网格上训练这些scale，同时冻结所有E2M1代码。在固定的原生格式、scale网格、块布局和代码平面约束下，LoRA合并变为位精确恒等操作，合并产物保持代码不变性。方法还消除了训练中的weight-space直通估计器。

Result: 在4个模型和4个任务上，Scale-QLoRA与注重合并的QAT-LoRA均达到无精度损失，作者不声称两者存在精度优劣。两者结构性差异在于：QAT-LoRA通过量化器重新推导代码平面，而Scale-QLoRA精确保留代码平面。该差异在生命周期中体现：最近舍入实现在任务上相差约1个百分点，更极端的规则不匹配可使权重空间产物降至约0%（作为灵敏度边界而非部署频率报告）。保留代码平面还使训练速度提升3.9倍（8B稠密模型每步），并支持精确回滚、代码平面去重以及约125倍更快的纯scale任务切换。

Conclusion: Scale-QLoRA通过在4位microscaling格式中仅适配scale字段而冻结代码平面，实现无损且代码不变的LoRA合并，从根本上消除传统合并量化带来的脆弱性和精度损失，同时带来计算加速和生命周期的操作优势。其与QAT-LoRA精度持平但结构上更具鲁棒性和灵活性。

Abstract: Merging a LoRA adapter into its base model is standard deployment practice: it removes the runtime adapter's per-forward overhead and leaves a single standalone checkpoint any serving stack can load. On a native 4-bit microscaling checkpoint (NVFP4, MXFP4) that step stops being free. The merged weights must be written back through a quantizer, which re-derives the checkpoint's discrete E2M1 code plane (roughly 90% of the artifact's bytes), so the deployed artifact becomes coupled to one quantization convention, and every later code-touching event in its lifecycle can move it. Done naively the step is worse than fragile: it deletes the adaptation, by up to 39 pp, because against an already-on-grid base the reconstruction optimum is that base. Scale-QLoRA instead adapts only the native per-block scale field, trains those scales on the deployment grid, and freezes every E2M1 code. Within a fixed native format, scale grid, block layout and code plane, merging is then a bit-exact identity and the merged artifact is code-invariant. Across four models and four tasks, Scale-QLoRA and merge-aware QAT-LoRA are both accuracy-lossless, so we claim no accuracy ordering between them; they differ structurally, in that QAT-LoRA re-derives the code plane through a quantizer while Scale-QLoRA preserves it exactly. That difference is what the lifecycle prices: nearest-rounding implementations disagree by about a point on the measured task, and more extreme rule mismatches can drive the weight-space artifact to ~0%, which we report as a sensitivity bound rather than a deployment frequency. Preserving the code plane also drops the weight-space straight-through estimator from training (3.9x per step on the dense 8B model) and enables exact rollback, code-plane deduplication, and a ~125x faster scale-only task swap.

</details>


### [102] [A Calibrated Reflection Approach for Enhancing Confidence Estimation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04539)
*Umesh Bodhwani,Yuan Ling,Shujing Dong,Yarong Feng,Hongfei Li,Ayush Goyal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"校准反思"（Calibrated Reflection）框架，用于增强大语言模型（LLM）的置信度估计能力。该框架包含三大创新：最大置信度选择（MCS）方法、基于反思的提示机制和距离感知校准技术，并在HelpSteer2、Llama T-REx及专有对话数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署中的一个关键挑战是如何可靠地估计模型自身的置信度，以便系统判断何时信任模型输出、何时需要人工介入。现有方法在置信度估计方面缺乏系统性的校准机制，需要更可靠的方案。

Method: 提出Calibrated Reflection框架，包含三个创新组件：(1) 最大置信度选择（MCS）方法，全面评估所有可能标签上的置信度；(2) 基于反思的提示机制，增强推理可靠性；(3) 距离感知校准技术，考虑标签之间的序数关系。

Result: 在HelpSteer2、Llama T-REx及一个专有对话数据集上进行了评估，结果表明该框架在对话式和基于事实的分类任务上均表现出有效性和良好效果。

Conclusion: 该工作为开发可靠且良好校准的LLM置信度估计方法做出了贡献，有助于实现对模型信任与人工判断的明智决策。

Abstract: A critical challenge in deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) is developing reliable mechanisms to estimate their confidence, enabling systems to determine when to trust model outputs versus seek human intervention. We present a Calibrated Reflection approach for enhancing confidence estimation in LLMs, a framework that combines structured reasoning with distance-aware calibration technique. Our approach introduces three key innovations: (1) a Maximum Confidence Selection (MCS) method that comprehensively evaluates confidence across all possible labels, (2) a reflection-based prompting mechanism that enhances reasoning reliability, and (3) a distance-aware calibration technique that accounts for ordinal relationships between labels.
